问题：U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？
参考答案：U1700 的 VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3，FB_B0 接 VCC1V1，FB_B1 接 VCC1V8，VOUT_BST 接 VCC50，EN1 接 MCU_5V_EN。
实际回答：U1700 的每个引脚及其连接的网络如下（基于证据[1]的引脚映射列表）： - nINT → MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1 - EN1 → MCU_5V_EN - VIN_B0_1 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_1 → VCC3V3 - VANA → VCC3V3 - SW_BST → N19935658 - NRST → N19935946 - SDA(EN3) → MCU_SDA_P15.5 - SCL(EN2) → MCU_SCL_P15.4 - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - CLKIN(GPO2) → MCU_WD_INH - WDI → MCU_WDI_P13.3 - VOUT_BST → VCC50 - PG1(GPO1) → N19936014 - WD_RESET → TC367_RESET_N - SW_B1_1 → N19935668 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - SW_B0_1 → N19935443 - SW_B0_2 → N19935443 - VIN_B0_2 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_2 → VCC3V3 - SW_B1_2 → N19935668 - PAD → GND - AGND → GND - PGND_BST → GND - PGND_B1_2 → GND - PGND_B1_1 → GND - PGND_B0_2 → GND - PGND_B0_1 → GND **支撑证据**：证据[1]（circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1700）提供了上述所有引脚与网络的对应关系。器件实例信息（证据[8]）仅确认器件型号，不涉及引脚连接。未发现其他证据冲突。
评分正文：U1700 的每个引脚及其连接的网络如下（基于证据[1]的引脚映射列表）： - nINT → MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1 - EN1 → MCU_5V_EN - VIN_B0_1 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_1 → VCC3V3 - VANA → VCC3V3 - SW_BST → N19935658 - NRST → N19935946 - SDA(EN3) → MCU_SDA_P15.5 - SCL(EN2) → MCU_SCL_P15.4 - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - CLKIN(GPO2) → MCU_WD_INH - WDI → MCU_WDI_P13.3 - VOUT_BST → VCC50 - PG1(GPO1) → N19936014 - WD_RESET → TC367_RESET_N - SW_B1_1 → N19935668 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - SW_B0_1 → N19935443 - SW_B0_2 → N19935443 - VIN_B0_2 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_2 → VCC3V3 - SW_B1_2 → N19935668 - PAD → GND - AGND → GND - PGND_BST → GND - PGND_B1_2 → GND - PGND_B1_1 → GND - PGND_B0_2 → GND - PGND_B0_1 → GND **支撑证据**：证据[1]（circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1700）提供了上述所有引脚与网络的对应关系。器件实例信息（证据[8]）仅确认器件型号，不涉及引脚连接。未发现其他证据冲突。

问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：根据网络VCC3V3的网表连接，该网络上连接的主要芯片（IC类器件）包括：U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804。其中U1500和U1802可识别为TPS22918负载开关，其余芯片型号未在证据中明确。 **支撑证据**： - [1] 网表“Net VCC3V3”列出连接器件：U1500.VIN、U1600.VCCIN、U1700.VIN_B0_1/VIN_B1_1/VANA/VIN_B0_2/VIN_B1_2、U1800.VIN/MODE/SYNC、U1802.VIN、U1804.VIN。 - [5] 网表“Net VCC3V3_ETH”中U1500的VOUT和QOD引脚连接该网络，结合[16]中“TPS22918负载开关将VCC3V3转换为VCC3V3_ETH”，可确认U1500为TPS22918。 - [4] 网表“Net VCC3V3_EQ”中U1802的VOUT和QOD引脚连接该网络，结合[13]中“TPS22918负载开关VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ”，可确认U1802为TPS22918。 **缺失信息**：U1600、U1700、U1800、U1804的具体芯片型号或功能在提供证据中未直接说明。
评分正文：根据网络VCC3V3的网表连接，该网络上连接的主要芯片（IC类器件）包括：U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804。其中U1500和U1802可识别为TPS22918负载开关，其余芯片型号未在证据中明确。 **支撑证据**： - [1] 网表“Net VCC3V3”列出连接器件：U1500.VIN、U1600.VCCIN、U1700.VIN_B0_1/VIN_B1_1/VANA/VIN_B0_2/VIN_B1_2、U1800.VIN/MODE/SYNC、U1802.VIN、U1804.VIN。 - [5] 网表“Net VCC3V3_ETH”中U1500的VOUT和QOD引脚连接该网络，结合[16]中“TPS22918负载开关将VCC3V3转换为VCC3V3_ETH”，可确认U1500为TPS22918。 - [4] 网表“Net VCC3V3_EQ”中U1802的VOUT和QOD引脚连接该网络，结合[13]中“TPS22918负载开关VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ”，可确认U1802为TPS22918。 **缺失信息**：U1600、U1700、U1800、U1804的具体芯片型号或功能在提供证据中未直接说明。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. 问题：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含哪些模块电路？
参考答案：HSI 2.3 节列出 KL30 电源管理、唤醒、电源、TC377 最小系统、EQ6 最小系统、摄像头最小系统、看门狗、温度采样、系统内电源电压采样、ETH、CAN/CAN FD 和 3V3 过压保护等电路。
实际回答：ADAS项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含以下模块电路：KL30电源管理电路、唤醒电路、电源电路、TC377最小系统、EQ6最小系统、摄像头最小系统、看门狗电路、温度采样电路、系统内电源电压采样电源、ETH电路、CAN/CAN FD电路、3V3过压保护电路。 支撑证据：文档目录中“2.3 模块电路”明确列出上述12项子章节，包括“KL30电源管理电路”、“唤醒电路”、“电源电路”、“TC377最小系统”、“EQ6最小系统”、“摄像头最小系统”、“看门狗电路”、“温度采样电路”、“系统内电源电压采样电源”、“ETH电路”、“CAN/CAN FD电路”、“3V3过压保护电路”。[证据2] **缺失信息**：无。所有模块电路均有直接证据支持。
评分正文：ADAS项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含以下模块电路：KL30电源管理电路、唤醒电路、电源电路、TC377最小系统、EQ6最小系统、摄像头最小系统、看门狗电路、温度采样电路、系统内电源电压采样电源、ETH电路、CAN/CAN FD电路、3V3过压保护电路。 支撑证据：文档目录中“2.3 模块电路”明确列出上述12项子章节，包括“KL30电源管理电路”、“唤醒电路”、“电源电路”、“TC377最小系统”、“EQ6最小系统”、“摄像头最小系统”、“看门狗电路”、“温度采样电路”、“系统内电源电压采样电源”、“ETH电路”、“CAN/CAN FD电路”、“3V3过压保护电路”。[证据2] **缺失信息**：无。所有模块电路均有直接证据支持。

The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system The circuit and PCB are designed according to the original factory specifications. The pins used are MCU_ETH_MDIO_P21.3，MCU_ETH_MDC_P21.2，MCU_MII_TXD3_P11.0,MCU_MII_TXD2_P11.1,MCU_MII_TXD1_P11.2, MCU_MII_TXD0_P11.3, MCU_MII_TXCLK_P11.5, MCU_MII_TXDV_P11.6, MCU_MII_RXD3_P11.7, MCU_MII_RXD2_P11.8, MCU_MII_RXD1_P11.9, MCU_MII_RXD0_P11.10,MCU_MII_RXDV_P11.11,MCU_MII_RXCLK_P11.12, PHY_RESET_N_P22.3, MCU_ETH_WAKE_P21.5.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F06/F11</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS正常工作电压范围9~16VDC，系统需求需要对产品供电KL30进行电源管理，硬件电路对UBD电源网络进行ADC采样。 ADAS normal operating voltage range is 9~16VDC. System requirements require power management for the product power supply KL30. The hardware circuit performs ADC sampling on the UBD power supply.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>MCU_UB_SW_P00.10信号是由MCU I/O控制的数字信号，用于使能KL30电压采样功能。 MCU_UB_SW_P00.10 signal is a digital signal controlled by MCU I/O, used to enable KL30 voltage sampling function. UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. MCU的ADC采集到UBD后进行电源管理，当软件诊断到UBD电压范围异常时，MCU会关断EQ和其他负载工作电源，确保ADAS功能安全。 The ADC of MCU collects UBD for power management. When the software detects that UBD voltage range is abnormal, the MCU will turn off EQ and other load power supplies to ensure the functional safety of ADAS.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | Figure 10-24. Layout Example Figure 10-24 shows the top layer of one of the evaluation modules for this device. 问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。
实际回答：### 直接结论 1. **主PMIC型号**：LP87702。 2. **输出电压**：VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50。 3. **软件配置**：通过I2C总线进行配置，具体配置内容包括设置寄存器以实现电压输出使能、中断监控等，但详细的寄存器列表和初始化序列未提供。 ### 支撑证据 #### 1. 主PMIC型号：LP87702 - **证据**：文档表格中明确列出“F13 POWER LP87702，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出”。（来源：**document:44c4021cbbe6e903**） - **证据**：电路图数据显示，名为“Power/PMIC”的模块组（Group 15、17、18）之间存在连接，且与MCU的I2C接口（MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5）和中断信号（MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1）相连，这与LP87702的典型接口一致。（来源：**circuit:21:net:MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1**， **circuit:21:module_connection:Group 17 · Power/PMIC:Group 18 · Power/PMIC:MCU_SCL_P15.4**， **circuit:21:module_connection:Group 17 · Power/PMIC:Group 18 · Power/PMIC:MCU_SDA_P15.5**） #### 2. 输出电压：VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50 - **证据**：文档表格“F13 POWER LP87702”的功能定义明确为“VCC3V3电压输入，VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出”。（来源：**document:44c4021cbbe6e903**， **document:e6627ad70c8cab97**） - **证据**：文档中描述LP87702的硬件接口时指出：“1、LP87702实现VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50三路电源输出”。（来源：**document:a6f318d1053a4c7e**） #### 3. 软件配置：通过I2C配置寄存器 - **证据**：文档中明确说明：“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：”。（来源：**document:d69d04d302326d46**） - **证据**：文档在描述LP87702的软件配置时，涉及对寄存器进行设置，例如表格中包含了“VMON_LDO1_OV_FS_REACTION”等寄存器的配置选项。（来源：**document:0987620a09f7a3bf**） - **证据**：文档中硬件接口描述指出：“LP87702的VCC50由MCU的GPIO控制使能和关断，VCC1V8、VCC1V1由MCU的I2C控制使能和关断。”以及“MCU可以通过I2C监控和管理LP87702的状态”。（来源：**document:a6f318d1053a4c7e**） ### 缺失信息 - **软件配置的详细寄存器设置**：虽然有提及需要通过I2C配置寄存器，但提供的证据中**缺失**LP87702配置所需的完整寄存器映射、初始化序列、以及具体的寄存器值。
评分正文：### 直接结论 1. **主PMIC型号**：LP87702。 2. **输出电压**：VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50。 3. **软件配置**：通过I2C总线进行配置，具体配置内容包括设置寄存器以实现电压输出使能、中断监控等，但详细的寄存器列表和初始化序列未提供。 ### 支撑证据 #### 1. 主PMIC型号：LP87702 - **证据**：文档表格中明确列出“F13 POWER LP87702，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出”。（来源：**document:44c4021cbbe6e903**） - **证据**：电路图数据显示，名为“Power/PMIC”的模块组（Group 15、17、18）之间存在连接，且与MCU的I2C接口（MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5）和中断信号（MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1）相连，这与LP87702的典型接口一致。（来源：**circuit:21:net:MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1**， **circuit:21:module_connection:Group 17 · Power/PMIC:Group 18 · Power/PMIC:MCU_SCL_P15.4**， **circuit:21:module_connection:Group 17 · Power/PMIC:Group 18 · Power/PMIC:MCU_SDA_P15.5**） #### 2. 输出电压：VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50 - **证据**：文档表格“F13 POWER LP87702”的功能定义明确为“VCC3V3电压输入，VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出”。（来源：**document:44c4021cbbe6e903**， **document:e6627ad70c8cab97**） - **证据**：文档中描述LP87702的硬件接口时指出：“1、LP87702实现VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50三路电源输出”。（来源：**document:a6f318d1053a4c7e**） #### 3. 软件配置：通过I2C配置寄存器 - **证据**：文档中明确说明：“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：”。（来源：**document:d69d04d302326d46**） - **证据**：文档在描述LP87702的软件配置时，涉及对寄存器进行设置，例如表格中包含了“VMON_LDO1_OV_FS_REACTION”等寄存器的配置选项。（来源：**document:0987620a09f7a3bf**） - **证据**：文档中硬件接口描述指出：“LP87702的VCC50由MCU的GPIO控制使能和关断，VCC1V8、VCC1V1由MCU的I2C控制使能和关断。”以及“MCU可以通过I2C监控和管理LP87702的状态”。（来源：**document:a6f318d1053a4c7e**） ### 缺失信息 - **软件配置的详细寄存器设置**：虽然有提及需要通过I2C配置寄存器，但提供的证据中**缺失**LP87702配置所需的完整寄存器映射、初始化序列、以及具体的寄存器值。

of Operation mode</td><td>TCAN1044A： 1、 运行模式 STB设置为低电平使能Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、 待机模式 STB设置为高电平使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据。 1.Run mode The STB is set to low to enable Normal mode, and the transceiver can send and receive data through the bus CANH and CANL. 2.Standby mode The STB is set to high level enable Standby mode, and the transceiver will no longer send the received data. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | None：U20; Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>模块序号 Module number</td><td>模块名称 Module name</td><td>功能定义 Function Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | 数量统计：4; ID：86; SM Name：Processor mechanism for SPF; SM TSR：The ECU shall integrate the processing platform safety mechanism for SPF._x000D_ When the processor SPF is confirmed, the ECU shall:_x000D_ 1. stop outputing ADAS request and;_x000D_ 2. disable the CAN communication and;_x000D_ 3. reset processing platform._x000D_ FHTI < 300 ms._x000D_ When the processor SPF is no longer detected after the processor reset, the ECU shall clear the processor SPF. ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_006; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：In initialization process, the SW shall configure SMU reset type to system reset. ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_018; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：The SW shall enable the lockstep. ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_020; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：软件应该避免PLFASH中出现全0或者全1。如果可能出现全0情况，软件应该使用0x36填充，不能写全1填充。 ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_021; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：软件应保证受CPU0 ENDINIT保护的寄存器被写操作时，正确解开CPU ENDINIT保护，写完寄存器后，再次锁上CPU ENDINIT保护。 解开CPU ENDINIT保护前，软件应屏蔽中断，防止写寄存器时间超时。 解开CPU ENDINIT保护后，写寄存器的时间小于1us ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_016; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：After SMU reset, the SW shall read the SMU fault status register and store SMU fault DTC and Snapshot in NVM. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. 问题：VCC0V75 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：**VCC0V75 电源由 TPS62872 芯片产生，输入为 VCC3V3，文档中未明确列出具体的电压、电流、纹波等配置要求，仅提及通过 I2C 通信设置输出电压。** --- ### 1. VCC0V75 电源由哪颗芯片产生？ **结论：** 由 TI 的 TPS62872 电源芯片产生。 **证据：** - 文档[4] 描述：“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75...现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。” - 文档[6] 描述：“采用TPS62872电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75，提供给EQ6核电供电。” - 文档[9] 的硬件接口列表中，将 TPS62872 与 VCC0V75 电源关联，并说明其由 MCU 的 P22.0 引脚（MCU_VCC0V75_EN）控制使能。 ### 2. 该芯片的输入电源是什么？ **结论：** 输入电源是 VCC3V3。 **证据：** - 文档[6] 描述：“采用TPS62872电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75”。 - 文档[4] 同样描述：“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75...”。 - 文档[8] 的模块功能表中明确列出：“F14. POWER...TPS62872，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC0V75电压输出”。 ### 3. 文档中对 VCC0V75 电源有什么配置要求？ **结论：** 文档中没有提供 VCC0V75 电源的电压、电流、纹波等具体参数要求。仅提及以下相关信息： - 输出电压通过 I2C 通信设置（文档[4]、[6]）。 - 使能控制：由 MCU 的 P22.0 引脚（MCU_VCC0V75_EN）控制（文档[9]）。 - 运行模式：在使能信号为高时正常输出 VCC0V75（文档[15]）。 - 采样精度要求：文档[13] 提到 MCU 对 VCC0V75 进行 ADC 采样，精度要求为 0.75V ± 3%（或 0.75V ± 3.3%），但这并非对电源芯片本身的输出配置要求，而是对采样电路的精度要求。 **缺失信息：** 文档中**缺失** VCC0V75 电源的输出电压标称值、输出电流能力、纹波等关键配置要求。
评分正文：**VCC0V75 电源由 TPS62872 芯片产生，输入为 VCC3V3，文档中未明确列出具体的电压、电流、纹波等配置要求，仅提及通过 I2C 通信设置输出电压。** --- ### 1. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F14</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的方案TPS62872。 VCC3V3 to VCCOV75 is applied to the kernel power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | None：MCU采样EQ6l系统的VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR电源电压 精度要求：0V75±5%; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：W4; None：AN18; None：AN18/P40.11/SENT_SENT11A/EVADC_G11CH8/EVADC_G2CH2; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：0V75_MIPI_DDR_ACQ; None：/; None：I; None：0V75_MIPI_DDR电压采样 U0V75_MIPI_DDR_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 硬件滤波截止频率：≥121.7Hz 硬件滤波时间：≤1.6ms 精度要求：0.75V±5%; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 None：MCU采样摄像头系统的3.3V电源电压，精度要求：3V3±5%; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：T7; None：AN12; None：AN12/EVADC_G1CH4/EDSADC_EDS0PB; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：VCC3V3_CAM_ACQ; None：/; None：I; None：索尼摄像头模组电源3.3V电压采样。 CAM 3V3电压采样 U3V3_CAM_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 U3V3_CAM=U3V3_CAM_ACQ/分压比，分压比=1/2 精度要求：3.3V±5%; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 None：MCU采样摄像头的1V1电源电压，精度要求：1~1.2V; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：W6; None：AN13; None：AN13/EVADC_G1CH5/EDSADC_EDS0NB; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：VCC1V1_CAM_ACQ; None：/; None：I; None：索尼摄像头模组电源1.1V电压采样。 U1V1_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 精度：1~1.2V 硬件滤波截止频率：≥121.7Hz 硬件滤波时间：≤1.6ms; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F05</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>Camera实现图像采集功能，根据ADAS系统需求，选择图像传感器芯片IMX-728作为camera主芯片。 The Camera realizes image acquisition function. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN NRST电压低于阈值。LP87702-Q1器件停止工作。 READ OTP 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO电平，并且NRST电压高于阈值电平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平和偏置电路被启用。OTP配置被加载到寄存器。 STANDBY 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平、控制和偏压电路被启用。所有的寄存器都可以被主机处理器通过系统串口读写。电压转换器可以根据需要进行使能输出。 ACTIVE 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。稳压DC/DC变换器处于使能状态。所有的寄存器都可以被MCU通过系统串口读写。 SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table>

问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节说明 VCC3V3_MCU 存在 3V3 过压保护电路：当 VCC3V3 超过 4.03V 时，比较器输出关闭 NMOS 与 PMOS，切断 VCC3V3_SW；EDF 可补充网络与二极管或 TVS 拓扑，但不能据此断言未被文档直接描述的保护能力。
实际回答：**结论**：3.3V电源（VCC3V3_MCU）存在过压保护，由外部硬件过压保护电路（HM ID F18）实现，保护阈值为4.03V。 **支撑证据**： - 设计文档明确列出模块F18“overvoltage protect”，用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护，功能安全等级ASIL-B[5]。 - 该模块的详细方案描述：当VCC3V3 > 4.03V时，cathode引脚输出逻辑低（最高1V），NMOS关闭，PMOS关闭，VCC3V3_SW无电，对后级负载起到过压保护作用[7]。 - 系统模块列表中包含“3V3过压保护电路”模块[4]。 **缺失信息**：无。
评分正文：**结论**：3.3V电源（VCC3V3_MCU）存在过压保护，由外部硬件过压保护电路（HM ID F18）实现，保护阈值为4.03V。 **支撑证据**： - 设计文档明确列出模块F18“overvoltage protect”，用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护，功能安全等级ASIL-B[5]。 - 该模块的详细方案描述：当VCC3V3 > 4.03V时，cathode引脚输出逻辑低（最高1V），NMOS关闭，PMOS关闭，VCC3V3_SW无电，对后级负载起到过压保护作用[7]。 - 系统模块列表中包含“3V3过压保护电路”模块[4]。 **缺失信息**：无。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. When VCC3V3 <4.03V, the cathode pin is in the high resistance state. clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | No.：86; Alarm ID：ALM9[0]; Alarm name：Temperature overflow; Related SMs：SM[HW]:DTS:TEMPERATURE_MONITOR; Safety related ?：Y; READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | Figure 10-1. 问题：板上用 TPS22918 做了哪些电源开关？逐个给出输入、输出和使能。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8 到 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：板上共有3个TPS22918电源开关实例，分别为U1500、U1802、U1803。每个实例的输入、输出和使能控制信号如下： - **U1500** - 输入：VCC3V3 - 输出：VCC3V3_ETH - 使能：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN - **U1802** - 输入：VCC3V3 - 输出：VCC3V3_EQ - 使能：MCU_3V3_EQ_EN - **U1803** - 输入：VCC1V8 - 输出：VCC1V8_EQ - 使能：MCU_1V8_EQ_EN **证据来源**： - 电路图引脚映射文件（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中U1500、U1802、U1803的pin_mapping分别记录了VIN、VOUT、ON的对应网络（证据[1][2][3]）。 - 设计文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中F16、F17、F19模块描述分别对应上述三个TPS22918的输入输出及使能信号（证据[7][8][9][11]）。 **缺失信息**：无。
评分正文：板上共有3个TPS22918电源开关实例，分别为U1500、U1802、U1803。每个实例的输入、输出和使能控制信号如下： - **U1500** - 输入：VCC3V3 - 输出：VCC3V3_ETH - 使能：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN - **U1802** - 输入：VCC3V3 - 输出：VCC3V3_EQ - 使能：MCU_3V3_EQ_EN - **U1803** - 输入：VCC1V8 - 输出：VCC1V8_EQ - 使能：MCU_1V8_EQ_EN **证据来源**： - 电路图引脚映射文件（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中U1500、U1802、U1803的pin_mapping分别记录了VIN、VOUT、ON的对应网络（证据[1][2][3]）。 - 设计文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中F16、F17、F19模块描述分别对应上述三个TPS22918的输入输出及使能信号（证据[7][8][9][11]）。 **缺失信息**：无。

评分上下文：4/14 段，1519/9733 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-multihop-eth-powersuccess0.3680.0000.500not_applicable0.3330.0001.0000.750not_applicable
问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？
参考答案：供电链路为 VCC3V3 → U1500（TPS22918，ON=MCU_3V3_ETH_EN）→ VCC3V3_ETH → U1501（TPS74501 LDO）→ VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：直接结论：以太网PHY的1.0V电源由U1501（稳压器）从VCC3V3_ETH转换产生，输出网络为VCC1V0_ETH。但无法确认该网络直接连接至PHY芯片的1.0V供电引脚，且PHY的具体型号及1.0V电源规格缺乏证据。 支撑结论的证据： - **供电级联路径**：U1501的输入引脚IN和使能引脚EN均连接至VCC3V3_ETH，输出引脚OUT连接至VCC1V0_ETH网络（证据14）。VCC1V0_ETH网络包含U1501.OUT、多个电容（C1420、C1506、C1507）、电感L1403、电阻R1146、R1504及测试点TP1503（证据19）。 - **PHY器件标识**：U1400的引脚映射显示其为PHY（连接MDI信号和复位/唤醒信号），但未提供其型号或1.0V电源引脚（证据15、16、17、18）。VCC1V0_ETH网络未直接连接到U1400的任何引脚。 缺失信息（缺口）： 1. **PHY型号**：U1400的器件型号未在证据中给出。 2. **1.0V电源规格**：PHY对1.0V电源的电流、纹波等要求无任何证据。 3. **供电连接确认**：无直接证据表明VCC1V0_ETH网络连接至U1400的1.0V电源引脚。现有证据仅显示U1400的信号引脚（MDI、复位、唤醒），电源引脚连接完全缺失。
评分正文：直接结论：以太网PHY的1.0V电源由U1501（稳压器）从VCC3V3_ETH转换产生，输出网络为VCC1V0_ETH。但无法确认该网络直接连接至PHY芯片的1.0V供电引脚，且PHY的具体型号及1.0V电源规格缺乏证据。 支撑结论的证据： - **供电级联路径**：U1501的输入引脚IN和使能引脚EN均连接至VCC3V3_ETH，输出引脚OUT连接至VCC1V0_ETH网络（证据14）。VCC1V0_ETH网络包含U1501.OUT、多个电容（C1420、C1506、C1507）、电感L1403、电阻R1146、R1504及测试点TP1503（证据19）。 - **PHY器件标识**：U1400的引脚映射显示其为PHY（连接MDI信号和复位/唤醒信号），但未提供其型号或1.0V电源引脚（证据15、16、17、18）。VCC1V0_ETH网络未直接连接到U1400的任何引脚。 缺失信息（缺口）： 1. **PHY型号**：U1400的器件型号未在证据中给出。 2. **1.0V电源规格**：PHY对1.0V电源的电流、纹波等要求无任何证据。 3. **供电连接确认**：无直接证据表明VCC1V0_ETH网络连接至U1400的1.0V电源引脚。现有证据仅显示U1400的信号引脚（MDI、复位、唤醒），电源引脚连接完全缺失。

MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | Sheet名称：0_Instruction Manual; 内容摘要：使用说明; 项目适配说明：nan ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：1_Face; 内容摘要：文档封面页; 项目适配说明：需要根据实际项目更新编号， 明确并修改xxx的型号 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：2_Sign; 内容摘要：本文档的当前状态、编制、审核和批准人员的信息; 项目适配说明：需要根据具体项目，更新文档 的当前状态，编制、审核和批准人员的信息等 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：3_Overview; 内容摘要：说明本文档的目的，适用对象和应用范围; 项目适配说明：无须确认和更新，仅用于了解 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：4_Change History1; 内容摘要：本文档模板的变更履历; 项目适配说明：填写本文档模板的变更内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：5_Change History2; 内容摘要：结合实际项目，安全机制的变更履历; 项目适配说明：填写本文档安全机制的变更内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：6_Reference Document; 内容摘要：导出安全机制的参考文档; 项目适配说明：根据实际项目，需参考最新的 数据手册和安全手册 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：7_Definition&Arch; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品的定义及架构; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在开发需求之前了解xxx系列产品的定义及架构 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：8_SG&FSR; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品的假设 安全目标和FSR; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在开发需求 前了解xxx系列产品的安全目标和FSR ——0_Instruction Manual | In standalone (nonstacked) applications, the user can disable the active pulldown of the EN pin if the user sets SINGLE = 1 in the CONTROL3 register. Fault conditions have no effect on the EN pin when SINGLE = 1 (the EN pin is always pulled down during device initialization). In stacked applications, ensure that SINGLE = 0. When the internal SYSTEM_READY signal is low (that is, initialization is complete and there are no fault conditions), the internal open-drain transistor is high impedance and the EN pin functions like a standard input. A high level on the EN pin enables the DC/DC converter in the device. A low level disables the DC/DC converter (the I2C interface is enabled as soon as the device has completed its initialization and is not affected by the state of the internal ENABLE or SYSTEM_READY signals). A low level on the EN pin forces the device into shutdown. During shutdown, the MOSFETs in the power stage are off, the internal control circuitry is disabled, and the device consumes only 20 μA (typical). The rising threshold voltage of the EN pin is 1.0 V and the falling threshold voltage is 0.9 V. The tolerance of the threshold voltages is ±30 mV, which means that the user can use the EN pin to implement precise turn-on and turn-off behavior. When power is applied to the VIN pin, the toggling of the EN pin does not reset the loaded default register settings. 9.3.4 Start-Up When the voltage on the VIN pin exceeds the positive-going UVLO threshold, the device initializes as follows: ·The device pulls the EN pin low. ·The device enables the internal reference voltage. · The device reads the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. · The device loads the default values into the device registers. When initialization is complete, the device enables I²C communication and releases the EN pin. The external circuitry controlling the EN pin now determines the behavior of the device: · If the EN pin is low, the device is disabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers, but the DC/DC converter does not operate · If the EN pin is high, the device is enabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers and, after a short delay, the DC/DC converter starts to ramp up its output. 问题：主控 MCU 是哪颗，型号是什么？
参考答案：EDF 中 U900 为 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，即 AURIX TC367；HSI 章节名称写作 TC377 最小系统。回答应披露 TC367 与 TC377 的来源差异，不能合并为无冲突的单一型号。
实际回答：主控 MCU 的型号存在证据冲突，无法确定单一型号。 **冲突详情** - 来源 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`（电路设计）中，实例 U900A~U900E 均为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S` 系列（不同后缀），该设计中的网络连接（如 `CAN1_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS4_P00.2` 连接至 `U900D.P00.2`）表明该电路板使用的 MCU 为 TC367 系列。 - 来源 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`（文档）中，明确写明“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有 SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA 主控芯片”，并多次将该芯片标识为 F01/MCU 模块的主控。 两个来源对同一字段给出不同取值，故不能合并为确定结论。
评分正文：主控 MCU 的型号存在证据冲突，无法确定单一型号。 **冲突详情** - 来源 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`（电路设计）中，实例 U900A~U900E 均为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S` 系列（不同后缀），该设计中的网络连接（如 `CAN1_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS4_P00.2` 连接至 `U900D.P00.2`）表明该电路板使用的 MCU 为 TC367 系列。 - 来源 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`（文档）中，明确写明“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有 SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA 主控芯片”，并多次将该芯片标识为 F01/MCU 模块的主控。 两个来源对同一字段给出不同取值，故不能合并为确定结论。

NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. 问题：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是谁？说清楚输入、三路输出和软件配置出处。
参考答案：U1700 LP87702-Q1 从 VCC3V3 取电，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50，软件配置出处为 HSI 7.2 节。
实际回答：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是 **LP87702**。 - **输入电压**：VCC3V3（3.3V）。证据来源：文档[15]（模块F13：LP87702，VCC3V3电压输入）和文档[12]（VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50）。 - **三路输出**：VCC1V1（1.1V）、VCC1V8（1.8V）、VCC50（5.0V）。证据来源：文档[15]（模块F13：VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出）和文档[12]（VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电）。 - **软件配置出处**：出自文档[9]中的“LP87702配置”部分，具体为“Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”。证据来源：文档[9]（LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器，如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration）。

READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | Register：nan; Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F20/F21/F22/F23</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>运行模式、待机模式、Sleep模式、OFF模式、Overtemp模式 Run mode, standby mode, Sleep mode, OFF mode, Overtemp mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>TCAN1044A： 1、 运行模式 STB设置为低电平使能Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、 待机模式 STB设置为高电平使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据。 1.Run mode The STB is set to low to enable Normal mode, and the transceiver can send and receive data through the bus CANH and CANL. 2.Standby mode The STB is set to high level enable Standby mode, and the transceiver will no longer send the received data. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F14</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的方案TPS62872。 VCC3V3 to VCCOV75 is applied to the kernel power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. is resistor-selectable through the FSEL pin. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | ID：SWRS_TC3XX_030; SM Name：SMC[SW]:MCU:LBIST_CFG; SM TSR：In initialization process, the SW shall configure LBIST parameters via LBISTCTRL0, LBISTCTRL1, LBISTCTRL2 and start LBIST execution. This register returns the device status flags. 10 Application and Implementation Note Information in the following applications sections is not part of the Tl component specification, and Tl does not warrant its accuracy or completeness. Tl's customers are responsible for determining suitability of components for their purposes, as well as validating and testing their design implementation to confirm systemfunctionality. 10.1 Application Information The following section discusses selection of the external components to complete the power supply design for typical a application. 10.2 Typical Application 10.2.1 Design Requirements Preliminary Calculations The maximum allowable deviation of the power supply is ±3.3%. The DC accuracy of the TPS6287x-Q1 is specified as ±1%, therefore, the maximum output voltage variation during a transient is given by: VoUT = ± VouT × (3.3% - 1%) = ±17.25 mV 10.2.2 Detailed Design Procedure The following subsections describe how to calculate the external components required to meet the specified transient requirements of a given application. The calculations include the worst-case variation of components and use the RMS method to combine the variation of uncorrelated parameters. 10.2.2.1 Selecting the Inductor The TPS6287x-Q1 devices have been optimized for inductors in the range 50 nH to 300 nH. If the transient response of the converter is limited by the slew rate of the current in the inductor, using a smaller inductor can improve performance. However, the output ripple current increases as the value of the inductor decreases and higher output current ripple generates higher output voltage ripple, which adds to the transient overshoot or undershoot. The optimal configuration for a given application is always a trade-off between a number of parameters. Tl recommends a starting value of 110 nH for typical applications. | all operating conditions. 问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：根据现有证据，U1800（TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1）的输入、输出检测、使能和I²C控制网络信息如下： * **输入网络**：VCC3V3（连接到VIN引脚）。 * **输出检测网络**：输出检测通过COMP引脚（网络N19809013）实现，同时VOSNS（网络VCC0V75）和GOSNS（接GND）用于远程输出电压检测。 * **使能网络**：EN引脚，连接到网络N19888813。 * **I²C控制网络**：SDA引脚连接到MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL引脚连接到MCU_SCL_P15.4。I²C设备地址为0x41，通信速率230KHz。 **支撑证据**： * U1800的引脚映射表明，其VIN引脚连接至VCC3V3，EN引脚连接至N19888813，SDA连接至MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL连接至MCU_SCL_P15.4，COMP引脚连接至N19809013，VOSNS连接至VCC0V75，GOSNS连接至GND（证据[1]）。 * U1800的器件型号为TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1（证据[2]）。 * 文档表明，MCU通过I²C总线配置TPS62872，其I²C地址为0x41，速率为230KHz（证据[3]）。
评分正文：根据现有证据，U1800（TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1）的输入、输出检测、使能和I²C控制网络信息如下： * **输入网络**：VCC3V3（连接到VIN引脚）。 * **输出检测网络**：输出检测通过COMP引脚（网络N19809013）实现，同时VOSNS（网络VCC0V75）和GOSNS（接GND）用于远程输出电压检测。 * **使能网络**：EN引脚，连接到网络N19888813。 * **I²C控制网络**：SDA引脚连接到MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL引脚连接到MCU_SCL_P15.4。I²C设备地址为0x41，通信速率230KHz。 **支撑证据**： * U1800的引脚映射表明，其VIN引脚连接至VCC3V3，EN引脚连接至N19888813，SDA连接至MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL连接至MCU_SCL_P15.4，COMP引脚连接至N19809013，VOSNS连接至VCC0V75，GOSNS连接至GND（证据[1]）。 * U1800的器件型号为TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1（证据[2]）。 * 文档表明，MCU通过I²C总线配置TPS62872，其I²C地址为0x41，速率为230KHz（证据[3]）。
