The output of the window comparator is deglitched - the typical deglitch time is 40 μs - and then used to drive the open-drain PG pin. During DVS activity, when the DC/DC converter transitions from one output voltage setting to another, the output voltage can temporarily exceed the limits of the window comparator and pull the PG pin low. The device has a feature to disable this behavior. If PGBLNKDVS = 1 in the CONTROL3 register, the device ignores the output of the power-good window comparator while DvS is active. Note that the PG pin is always low, regardless of the output of the window comparator, when: · The device is in thermal shutdown. The device is disabled. The device is in undervoltage lockout.· The device is in soft start. 9.3.14.2 Secondary Device Behavior initialization, the device presets FF1 and FF2, which pulls down the PG pin and forces the device to operate in DCM. When the device completes its soft start, it resets FF2, which turns off Q1. However, in a stacked configuration, all devices share the same PG signal, and therefore the PG pin stays low until all devices in the stack have completed their soft start. When that happens, FF1 is reset and the converters operate in CCM. 9.3.15 Remote Sense The device has two pins, VOSNS and GOSNS, to remotely sense the output voltage. Remote sensing lets the converter sense the output voltage directly at the point-of-load and increases the accuracy of the output voltage regulation. 9.3.16 Thermal Warning and Shutdown The device has a two-level overtemperature detection function. If the junction temperature rises above the thermal warning threshold of 150°C (typical), the device sets the TWARN bit in the STATUS register. The device clears the TWARN bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TWARN threshold of 130°C (typical). If the junction temperature rises above the thermal shutdown threshold of 170°C (typical), the device: · Stops switching · Pulls down the EN pin (if SINGLE = 0 in the CONTROL3 register) · Enables the output discharge (if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register) ·Sets the TSHUT bit in the STATUS register · Pulls the PG pin low | ECU Connector：X1900-15 X1900-16; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | None：MCU外挂20MHz晶体; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：M20; None：XTAL1; None：XTAL /VEXT; None：CLOCK; None：——; None：/; None：I; None：MCU的时钟信号输入; None：I; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：M19; None：XTAL2; None：XTAL /VEXT; None：CLOCK; None：——; None：/; None：O; None：MCU的时钟驱动信号输出; None：O; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 HW Interface Component ：QM; None：F09/ETH and CAN power samp2; None：MCU采样CAN的VCC50电源电压，精度要求：5V±5%; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：U10; None：AN1; None：AN1/EVADC_G0CH1/EDSADC_EDS3NA; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：VCC50_ACQ; None：/; None：I; None：5V电压采样 U5V0_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 U5V0=U5V0_ACQ/分压比，分压比=1/2 精度要求：5V±10%; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 None：MCU采样以太网的VCC1V0_ETH电源电压 精度要求：0.9V～1.1V （仅当使用千兆以太网时采样）; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：U7; None：AN9; None：AN9/EVADC_G11CH5/EVADC_G1CH1; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：1V0_ETH_ACQ; None：以太网1V0电压采样 U1V1_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 精度：0.95~1.1V 硬件滤波截止频率：≥121.7Hz 硬件滤波时间：≤1.6ms; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Table9-5.DefaultOutputVoltageSetpoints</caption> <tr><td >VSEL Pin(1)</td><td >Device Number</td><td >VSET[7:0]</td><td >Output Voltage Setpoint</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287x-Q1</td><td >0x5a</td><td >850 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY0-Q1</td><td >0x50</td><td >800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY1-Q1</td><td >0x14</td><td >500 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY2-Q1</td><td >0x64</td><td >1800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td >6.2 kQ to GND</td><td >TPS6287xY3-Q1</td><td >0x52</td><td >810 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN0-Q1</td><td >0x14</td><td >500 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN1-Q1</td><td >0x50</td><td >800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN2-Q1</td><td >0x64</td><td >1800 mV</td></tr> </table> | ECU Connector：X2000-60; For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. 问题：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过哪颗负载开关，使能信号是什么？
参考答案：VCC1V8 经 U1803（TPS22918）输出为 VCC1V8_EQ，使能信号为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：VCC1V8到EQ的1.8V电源经过的负载开关是型号为TPS22918的器件（对应设计编号U1803），其使能信号为MCU_1V8_EQ_EN，由MCU的P20.0引脚输出。 **证据支持：** 1. **负载开关器件**：电路设计拓扑显示，VCC1V8通过U1803（20181204001）输出VCC1V8_EQ，控制信号为MCU_1V8_EQ_EN（来源：电路设计[1]）。技术文档明确说明，该负载开关方案采用TPS22918芯片，将VCC1V8转换为VCC1V8_EQ，为EQ6供电（来源：文档[14]、[15]）。模块清单也标注F19模块为“TPS22918 负载开关，VCC1V8电压输入，VCC1V8_EQ电压输出”（来源：文档[17]）。 2. **使能信号**：硬件接口文档描述，F19模块（TPS22918）的使能信号为MCU_1V8_EQ_EN，由MCU的P20.0引脚控制，高电平使能VCC1V8_EQ输出（来源：文档[20]）。功能描述文档也确认，MCU通过将MCU_1V8_EQ_EN信号配置为逻辑高来使能VCC1V8_EQ电源输出（来源：文档[12]）。 **缺失信息：** 无。所有子问题均有直接证据覆盖，无冲突。
评分正文：VCC1V8到EQ的1.8V电源经过的负载开关是型号为TPS22918的器件（对应设计编号U1803），其使能信号为MCU_1V8_EQ_EN，由MCU的P20.0引脚输出。 **证据支持：** 1. **负载开关器件**：电路设计拓扑显示，VCC1V8通过U1803（20181204001）输出VCC1V8_EQ，控制信号为MCU_1V8_EQ_EN（来源：电路设计[1]）。技术文档明确说明，该负载开关方案采用TPS22918芯片，将VCC1V8转换为VCC1V8_EQ，为EQ6供电（来源：文档[14]、[15]）。模块清单也标注F19模块为“TPS22918 负载开关，VCC1V8电压输入，VCC1V8_EQ电压输出”（来源：文档[17]）。 2. **使能信号**：硬件接口文档描述，F19模块（TPS22918）的使能信号为MCU_1V8_EQ_EN，由MCU的P20.0引脚控制，高电平使能VCC1V8_EQ输出（来源：文档[20]）。功能描述文档也确认，MCU通过将MCU_1V8_EQ_EN信号配置为逻辑高来使能VCC1V8_EQ电源输出（来源：文档[12]）。 **缺失信息：** 无。所有子问题均有直接证据覆盖，无冲突。

Power off mode The MCU turns off the VCC1V8-EQ power output by configuring the MCU-1V8-EQ_EN signal to logic low. Run mode TPS22918 normal output VCC1V8_EQ.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F19</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>1V8转1V8_EQ用于EQ6供电，现选择负载开关方案。 1V8 to 1V8_EQ is used for the EQ6 power supply, now choose the load switch scheme. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC1V8电源转换为VCC1V8_EQ，给EQ6供电。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch chip is used to convert the VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ to power EQ6. The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | Typical ApplicationSchematic 110 nH±20% 0.75V V VIN SW 3.3V VIN MODE/SYNC 20 kQ C C VoO Load VOSNS EN GOSNS SDA SCL >Rz VSEL FSEL 3.3V Cc 6.2 kQ [cc M10 kQ COMP 1kQ OPG PG SYNC_OUT GND 10pF GND | In standalone (nonstacked) applications, the user can disable the active pulldown of the EN pin if the user sets SINGLE = 1 in the CONTROL3 register. Fault conditions have no effect on the EN pin when SINGLE = 1 (the EN pin is always pulled down during device initialization). In stacked applications, ensure that SINGLE = 0. When the internal SYSTEM_READY signal is low (that is, initialization is complete and there are no fault conditions), the internal open-drain transistor is high impedance and the EN pin functions like a standard input. A high level on the EN pin enables the DC/DC converter in the device. A low level disables the DC/DC converter (the I2C interface is enabled as soon as the device has completed its initialization and is not affected by the state of the internal ENABLE or SYSTEM_READY signals). A low level on the EN pin forces the device into shutdown. During shutdown, the MOSFETs in the power stage are off, the internal control circuitry is disabled, and the device consumes only 20 μA (typical). The rising threshold voltage of the EN pin is 1.0 V and the falling threshold voltage is 0.9 V. The tolerance of the threshold voltages is ±30 mV, which means that the user can use the EN pin to implement precise turn-on and turn-off behavior. When power is applied to the VIN pin, the toggling of the EN pin does not reset the loaded default register settings. 9.3.4 Start-Up When the voltage on the VIN pin exceeds the positive-going UVLO threshold, the device initializes as follows: ·The device pulls the EN pin low. ·The device enables the internal reference voltage. · The device reads the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. · The device loads the default values into the device registers. When initialization is complete, the device enables I²C communication and releases the EN pin. The external circuitry controlling the EN pin now determines the behavior of the device: · If the EN pin is low, the device is disabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers, but the DC/DC converter does not operate · If the EN pin is high, the device is enabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers and, after a short delay, the DC/DC converter starts to ramp up its output. The SSTIME[1:0] bits in the CONTROL2 register select the duration of the soft-start ramp: | Figure 9-8. Start-Up Timing When an External Signal is Connected to the EN Pin Figure 9-8 shows the start-up sequence when an external signal is connected to the EN pin. 问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：根据现有证据，文档声明了4个CAN相关模块，且网表可确认其具体连接。 **1. 文档中声明的CAN相关模块** 文档声明了以下4个CAN相关模块： * **CAN0**：基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯（模块编号F23）[13]。 * **CAN1**：基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯（模块编号F22）[13]。 * **CAN2**：基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯（模块编号F21）[13]。 * **CAN3**：基于TCAN1145的CAN通讯，硬件支持特定报文唤醒（模块编号F20）[13]。 **2. 使用网表确认的具体连接** 网表证据确认了CAN相关模块的以下具体网络连接： * **CAN0网络**：包括CAN0H、CAN0L、CAN0_INH、CAN0_INH_ACQ、CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8等网络，分别连接到特定元件引脚（如C1201、D1200、L1200、U1200、U1203、U900A等）[1][2][3][4][5]。 * **CAN1网络**：包括CAN1H、CAN1L、CAN1_INH等网络，分别连接到特定元件引脚（如C1207、D1201、L1201、U1204、U1205等）[6][7][8]。 * **CAN2网络**：包括CAN2H、CAN2L等网络，分别连接到特定元件引脚（如C1301、D1300、L1300等）[9][10]。 * **CAN3网络**：包括CAN3H、CAN3L等网络，分别连接到特定元件引脚（如C1306、D1301、L1301等）[11][12]。 **缺失信息** 无。
评分正文：根据现有证据，文档声明了4个CAN相关模块，且网表可确认其具体连接。 **1. 文档中声明的CAN相关模块** 文档声明了以下4个CAN相关模块： * **CAN0**：基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯（模块编号F23）[13]。 * **CAN1**：基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯（模块编号F22）[13]。 * **CAN2**：基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯（模块编号F21）[13]。 * **CAN3**：基于TCAN1145的CAN通讯，硬件支持特定报文唤醒（模块编号F20）[13]。 **2. 使用网表确认的具体连接** 网表证据确认了CAN相关模块的以下具体网络连接： * **CAN0网络**：包括CAN0H、CAN0L、CAN0_INH、CAN0_INH_ACQ、CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8等网络，分别连接到特定元件引脚（如C1201、D1200、L1200、U1200、U1203、U900A等）[1][2][3][4][5]。 * **CAN1网络**：包括CAN1H、CAN1L、CAN1_INH等网络，分别连接到特定元件引脚（如C1207、D1201、L1201、U1204、U1205等）[6][7][8]。 * **CAN2网络**：包括CAN2H、CAN2L等网络，分别连接到特定元件引脚（如C1301、D1300、L1300等）[9][10]。 * **CAN3网络**：包括CAN3H、CAN3L等网络，分别连接到特定元件引脚（如C1306、D1301、L1301等）[11][12]。 **缺失信息** 无。

When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | 顾客代表Customer Representative其他与项目相关人员等Other relevant person术语及缩写解释 Terms and AbbreviationsTable 1-1Terms and Abbreviations参考文档 Reference DocumentTable 1-2Reference Document硬件架构 Hardware Architecture硬件架构描述了60060894_ADAS项目的硬件基础模块，模块和模块之间的接口。The Hardware Architecture describe the HW basic component of 600608964_ADAS project, as well as the interface of each component.系统概述 System overviewADAS产品要求功能安全达到ASIL-B，硬件整体架构介绍如下：ADAS products require functional safety to meet ASIL-B. The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | Updated theDevice OptionsTable. 3 .5 Modified MODE/SYNC pin Description in Pin Functions Changed the title of Figure 8-5 12 Added approximate minimum on time value. 15 Added behavior of device when EN is toggled and input voltage is applied.. .17 · Modified Table 9-5 to reflect new available Orderable Part Number.. 21 Added a comment on how to connect secondary devices SYNC_OUT pin.. 27 Added a footnote to Table 9-10 .31 Added I2C Register Reset .34 Added Number of phases NΦ and adapted passive component selection equations in Typical Application ...41 Page Changes from Revision * (May 2022) to Revision A (July 2022) Changed document status from Advance Information to Production Data...... .1 5 Description (continued) The switching frequency is resistor-selectable through the FSEL pin. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. For more information, see Table 9-5 and (2) 7 Pin Configuration and Functions 8 Specifications 8.1 Absolute Maximum Ratings over operating temperature range (unless otherwise noted)(1) (1)Operation outside the Absolute Maximum Ratings may cause permanent device damage. Absolute Maximum Ratings do not imply functional operation of the device at these or any other conditions beyond those listed under Recommended Operating Conditions. If used outside the Recommended Operating Conditions but within the Absolute Maximum Ratings, the device may not be fully functional, and this may affect device reliability, functionality, performance, and shorten the device lifetime. (2)All voltage values are with respect to the GND pin. While switching. (3) | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | A stacked configuration uses a daisy-chained clocking signal, in which each device switches with a phase offset of approximately 140° relative to the adjacent devices in the daisy-chain. To daisy-chain the clocking signal, connect the SYNC_OUT pin of the primary device to the MODE/SYNC pin of the first secondary device. Connect the SYNC_OUT pin of the first secondary device to the MODE/SYNC pin of the second secondary device. Continue this connection scheme for all devices in the stack, to daisy-chain them together. Hiccup overcurrent protection must not be used in a stacked configuration. In a stacked configuration, the common enable signal also acts as a SYSTEM_READY signal (see Section 9.3.3). Each device in the stack can pull its EN pin low during device start-up or when a fault occurs. Thus, the stack is only enabled when all devices have completed their start-up sequence and are fault-free. A fault in any one device disables the whole stack for as long as the fault condition exists. During start-up, the primary converter pulls the COMP pin low for as long as the enable signal (SYSTEM_READY) is low. When the enable signal goes high, the primary device actively controls the COMP pin and all converters in the stack follow the COMP voltage. During start-up, each device in the stack pulls its PG pin low while it initializes. When initialization is complete, each secondary device in the stack sets its PG pin to a high impedance and the primary device alone controls the state of the PG signal. The PG pin goes high when the stack has completed its start-up ramp and the output voltage is within specification. The secondary converters in the stack detect the rising edge of the power-good signal and switch from DCM operation to CCM operation. After the stack has successfully started up, the primary device controls the power-good signal in the normal way. In a stacked configuration, there are some faults that only affect individual devices, and other faults that affect all devices. For example, if one device enters current limit, only that device is affected. But a thermal shutdown or undervoltage lockout event in one device disables all devices through the shared enable (SYSTEM_READY) signal. Functionality During Stacked Operation Some device features are not available during stacked operation, or are only available in the primary converter. Fault Handling During Stacked Operation In a stacked configuration, there are some faults that only affect individual devices and other faults that affect all devices. For example, if one device enters current limit, only that device is affected. A thermal shutdown or undervoltage lockout event in one device disables all devices through the shared enable (SYSTEM_READY) signal. Table 9-9 summarizes the fault handling of the TPS6287x-Q1 devices during stacked operation. | If the user applies a valid clock signal to the MODE/SYNC pin, the device synchronizes its switching cycles to the external clock and automatically selects forced PwM operation. The MODE/SYNC pin is logically ORed with the FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register (see Table 9-1). When multiple devices are used together in a stacked configuration, the MODE/SYNC pin of the secondary devices is the input for the clock signal (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.9 Spread Spectrum Clocking (SSC) The device has a spread spectrum clocking function that can reduce electromagnetic interference (EMl). When the SSC function is active, the device modulates the switching frequency to approximately ±10% the nominal value. The frequency modulation has a triangular characteristic (see Figure 9-11). To use the SSC function, make sure that: · SSCEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. · Forced PWM operation is selected (MODE pin is high or FPWMEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register). Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 ·The device is not synchronized to an external clock. To disable the SSC function, ensure that SCCEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. To use the SSC function with multiple devices in a stacked configuration, ensure that the primary converter runs from its internal oscillator and synchronize all secondary converters to the primary clock (see Figure 9-14). 9.3.10 Output Discharge The device has an output discharge function that ensures a defined ramp down of the output voltage when the device is disabled and keeps the output voltage close to 0 V while the device is off. The output discharge function is enabled when DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The output discharge function is enabled by default. If enabled, the device discharges the output under the following conditions: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = O in the CONTROL1 register. · A thermal shutdown event occurs. · A UVLO event occurs. · An OVLO event occurs. The output discharge function is not available until the user has enabled the device at least once after power up During power down, the device continues to discharge the output for as long as the internal supply voltage is greater than approximately 1.8 V. | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1

Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. </td><td></td><td >92</td><td >94</td><td >96</td><td >%VoUT </td></tr> <tr><td >VT+</td><td > Positive-going power good threshold voltage (output overvoltage)</td><td></td><td >104</td><td >106</td><td >108</td><td >%VouT</td></tr> <tr><td >VT-</td><td >Negative-going power good threshold voltage (output overvoltage)</td><td></td><td >102</td><td >104</td><td >106</td><td >%VoUT </td></tr> <tr><th >VoL</th><th >Low-level output voltage (PG)</th><th >loL = 1 mA</th><th></th><th></th><th >0.3</th><th></th></tr> </table> | In standalone (nonstacked) applications, the user can disable the active pulldown of the EN pin if the user sets SINGLE = 1 in the CONTROL3 register. Fault conditions have no effect on the EN pin when SINGLE = 1 (the EN pin is always pulled down during device initialization). In stacked applications, ensure that SINGLE = 0. When the internal SYSTEM_READY signal is low (that is, initialization is complete and there are no fault conditions), the internal open-drain transistor is high impedance and the EN pin functions like a standard input. A high level on the EN pin enables the DC/DC converter in the device. A low level disables the DC/DC converter (the I2C interface is enabled as soon as the device has completed its initialization and is not affected by the state of the internal ENABLE or SYSTEM_READY signals). A low level on the EN pin forces the device into shutdown. During shutdown, the MOSFETs in the power stage are off, the internal control circuitry is disabled, and the device consumes only 20 μA (typical). The rising threshold voltage of the EN pin is 1.0 V and the falling threshold voltage is 0.9 V. The tolerance of the threshold voltages is ±30 mV, which means that the user can use the EN pin to implement precise turn-on and turn-off behavior. When power is applied to the VIN pin, the toggling of the EN pin does not reset the loaded default register settings. 9.3.4 Start-Up When the voltage on the VIN pin exceeds the positive-going UVLO threshold, the device initializes as follows: ·The device pulls the EN pin low. ·The device enables the internal reference voltage. · The device reads the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. · The device loads the default values into the device registers. When initialization is complete, the device enables I²C communication and releases the EN pin. The external circuitry controlling the EN pin now determines the behavior of the device: · If the EN pin is low, the device is disabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers, but the DC/DC converter does not operate · If the EN pin is high, the device is enabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers and, after a short delay, the DC/DC converter starts to ramp up its output. The SSTIME[1:0] bits in the CONTROL2 register select the duration of the soft-start ramp: | 9.4.4 0n The device operates in on mode when the supply voltage is greater than the UVLO threshold and all of the following conditions are true: ·A high-level is applied to the EN pin. SWEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The device junction temperature is below the thermal shutdown threshold.· · The supply voltage is below the OVLO threshold. All functions are available in on mode. The device leaves on mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold. The device leaves on mode and enters standby mode when any of the following conditions is true: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. · The device junction temperature is greater than the thermal shutdown threshold. ·The supply voltage is greater than the OVLO threshold. 9.5 Programming 9.5.1 Serial Interface Description I2C is a 2-wire serial interface developed by Philips Semiconductor, now NXP Semiconductors (see I2C-Bus Specification and User Manual, Revision 6, 4 April 2014). The bus consists of a data line (SDA) and a clock line (SCL) with pullup structures. When the bus is idle, both SDA and SCL lines are pulled high. All I2C-compatible devices connect to the I2C bus through open-drain I/O pins, SDA and SCL. A controller, usually a microcontroller or a digital signal processor, controls the bus. The controller is responsible for generating the SCL signal and device addresses. The controller also generates specific conditions that indicate the START and STOP of data transfer. A target receives data, transmits data, or both on the bus under control of the controller. The TPS6287x-Q1 device operates as a target and supports the following data transfer modes, as defined in the I2C-Bus Specification: standard mode (100 kbps), fast mode (400 kbps), and fast mode plus (1 Mbps). The interface adds flexibility to the power supply solution, enabling most functions to be programmed to new values depending on the instantaneous application requirements. Register contents remain intact as long as the input voltage remains above 1.4 V. The data transfer protocol for standard and fast modes is exactly the same, therefore, they are referred to as F/S-mode in this document. The device supports 7-bit addressing; general call addresses are not supported. | 9.3.11 Undervoltage Lockout (UVLO) The device has an undervoltage lockout function that disables the device if the supply voltage is too low for correct operation. The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. The current limit in practice is given by: where: · I, is the inductor current. ·ILiMH is the high-side current limit threshold measured at DC Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | high level, LSB = 1. 9.3.6 Output Voltage Setting 9.3.6.1 Output Voltage Range The device has four different voltage ranges. The VRANGE[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register control which range is active (see Table 9-3). The default output voltage range after device initialization is 0.4 V to 1.675 V in 5-mV steps. Note that every change to the VRANGE[1:0] bits must be followed by a write to the VSET register, even if the value of the VSET[7:o] bits does not change. This sequence is required for the device to start to use the new voltage range. Also note that the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range uses a 0.8-V reference and the other ranges use a 0.4-V reference. Switching to and from the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range can therefore cause increased output voltage undershoot or overshoot while the device switches its internal reference. In device variants that do not have I?C, the output voltage range is factory-set to 0.4 V to 1.675 V. 9.3.6.2OutputVoltageSetpoint Together with the selected range, the VSET[7:0] bits in the VSET register control the output voltage setpoint of the device (see Table 9-4). During initialization, the device reads the state of the VSEL pin and selects the default output voltage according to Table 9-5. Note that the VSEL pin also selects the I?C target address of the device (see Table 9-10). If the user programs new output voltage setpoint (VOUT[7:0]), output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]), or soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings when the device has already begun its soft-start sequence, the device ignores the new values until the soft-start sequence is complete. If the user changes the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, ramps up or down to the new value. If the user changes VOUT[7:0], VRAMP[1:0], or SSTIME[1:0] while EN is low, the device uses the new values the next time the user enables it. | Figure 9-5. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | Sheet名称：0_Instruction Manual; 内容摘要：使用说明; 项目适配说明：nan ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：1_Face; 内容摘要：文档封面页; 项目适配说明：需要根据实际项目更新编号， 明确并修改xxx的型号 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：2_Sign; 内容摘要：本文档的当前状态、编制、审核和批准人员的信息; 项目适配说明：需要根据具体项目，更新文档 的当前状态，编制、审核和批准人员的信息等 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：3_Overview; 内容摘要：说明本文档的目的，适用对象和应用范围; 项目适配说明：无须确认和更新，仅用于了解 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：4_Change History1; 内容摘要：本文档模板的变更履历; 项目适配说明：填写本文档模板的变更内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：5_Change History2; 内容摘要：结合实际项目，安全机制的变更履历; 项目适配说明：填写本文档安全机制的变更内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：6_Reference Document; 内容摘要：导出安全机制的参考文档; 项目适配说明：根据实际项目，需参考最新的 数据手册和安全手册 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：7_Definition&Arch; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品的定义及架构; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在开发需求之前了解xxx系列产品的定义及架构 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：8_SG&FSR; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品的假设 安全目标和FSR; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在开发需求 前了解xxx系列产品的安全目标和FSR ——0_Instruction Manual | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. 问题：不要概括，逐颗列出所有 TPS22918 的输入电源、输出电源和 ON 控制。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3→VCC3V3_ETH，ON 为 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3→VCC3V3_EQ，ON 为 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8→VCC1V8_EQ，ON 为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：### 结论 以下逐颗列出所有 TPS22918 器件的输入电源、输出电源和 ON 控制引脚连接： 1. **U1500**（TPS22918，位号 U1500） - 输入电源（VIN）：VCC3V3 - 输出电源（VOUT）：VCC3V3_ETH - ON 控制（ON）：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN 2. **U1802**（TPS22918，位号 U1802） - 输入电源（VIN）：VCC3V3 - 输出电源（VOUT）：VCC3V3_EQ - ON 控制（ON）：MCU_3V3_EQ_EN 3. **U1803**（TPS22918，位号 U1803） - 输入电源（VIN）：VCC1V8 - 输出电源（VOUT）：VCC1V8_EQ - ON 控制（ON）：MCU_1V8_EQ_EN ### 支撑证据 - 证据[1]（pin_mapping U1500）：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_ETH，ON → MCU_3V3_ETH_EN - 证据[2]（pin_mapping U1802）：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_EQ，ON → MCU_3V3_EQ_EN - 证据[3]（pin_mapping U1803）：VIN → VCC1V8，VOUT → VCC1V8_EQ，ON → MCU_1V8_EQ_EN - 证据[5]、[6]、[7]（instance U1500、U1802、U1803）确认器件型号为 TPS22918 ### 缺失信息 无。上述三颗器件已完整覆盖，无其他 TPS22918 实例。
评分正文：### 结论 以下逐颗列出所有 TPS22918 器件的输入电源、输出电源和 ON 控制引脚连接： 1. **U1500**（TPS22918，位号 U1500） - 输入电源（VIN）：VCC3V3 - 输出电源（VOUT）：VCC3V3_ETH - ON 控制（ON）：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN 2. **U1802**（TPS22918，位号 U1802） - 输入电源（VIN）：VCC3V3 - 输出电源（VOUT）：VCC3V3_EQ - ON 控制（ON）：MCU_3V3_EQ_EN 3. **U1803**（TPS22918，位号 U1803） - 输入电源（VIN）：VCC1V8 - 输出电源（VOUT）：VCC1V8_EQ - ON 控制（ON）：MCU_1V8_EQ_EN ### 支撑证据 - 证据[1]（pin_mapping U1500）：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_ETH，ON → MCU_3V3_ETH_EN - 证据[2]（pin_mapping U1802）：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_EQ，ON → MCU_3V3_EQ_EN - 证据[3]（pin_mapping U1803）：VIN → VCC1V8，VOUT → VCC1V8_EQ，ON → MCU_1V8_EQ_EN - 证据[5]、[6]、[7]（instance U1500、U1802、U1803）确认器件型号为 TPS22918 ### 缺失信息 无。上述三颗器件已完整覆盖，无其他 TPS22918 实例。

问题：VCC3V3 在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电？不要把电阻电容当成主要负载。
参考答案：VCC3V3 是主要 3.3V 电源轨，供给 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600 等 IC；回答应聚焦芯片而非无源器件。
实际回答：VCC3V3 在板上主要给以下电源器件供电：TPS62872（DC-DC 转换器，输出 VCC0V75）、SA23002（DC-DC 转换器，输出 0V75_MIPI_DDR）、LP87702（PMIC，输出 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50）、TPS22918（负载开关，输出 VCC3V3_EQ），以及一个用于 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关（型号未明确）。 **支撑证据** - 文档明确列出 VCC3V3 为输入的电源转换方案： - VCC3V3 → VCC0V75 使用 TPS62872（[证据 11] 第 “VCC3V3 to VCC0V75” 段，[证据 16] 表 ) - VCC3V3 → 0V75_MIPI_DDR 使用 SA23002（[证据 11] 第 “VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR” 段，[证据 13] 表 ) - VCC3V3 → VCC1V8/VCC1V1/VCC50 使用 LP87702 PMIC（[证据 11] 第 “VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50” 段，[证据 19] 序号 1 表 ) - VCC3V3 → VCC3V3_EQ 使用 TPS22918 负载开关（[证据 11] 第 “VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ” 段，[证据 20] 表 ) - VCC3V3 → VCC3V3_ETH 使用负载开关方案（[证据 11] 第 “VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH” 段），但未给出具体型号。 - 原理图网络连接显示 VCC3V3 直接连接到上述电源器件的电源输入引脚（[证据 2] 中 U1800.VIN、U1804.VIN、U1700.VIN 等分别对应各方案，[证据 5] 中 U1802.VOUT 证实 VCC3V3_EQ 输出由 TPS22918 产生）。 **缺失信息** - 用于 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关型号未在文档中提供，仅知方案为负载开关，原理图中 U1500 可能承担该功能但无型号证据。
评分正文：VCC3V3 在板上主要给以下电源器件供电：TPS62872（DC-DC 转换器，输出 VCC0V75）、SA23002（DC-DC 转换器，输出 0V75_MIPI_DDR）、LP87702（PMIC，输出 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50）、TPS22918（负载开关，输出 VCC3V3_EQ），以及一个用于 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关（型号未明确）。 **支撑证据** - 文档明确列出 VCC3V3 为输入的电源转换方案： - VCC3V3 → VCC0V75 使用 TPS62872（[证据 11] 第 “VCC3V3 to VCC0V75” 段，[证据 16] 表 ) - VCC3V3 → 0V75_MIPI_DDR 使用 SA23002（[证据 11] 第 “VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR” 段，[证据 13] 表 ) - VCC3V3 → VCC1V8/VCC1V1/VCC50 使用 LP87702 PMIC（[证据 11] 第 “VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50” 段，[证据 19] 序号 1 表 ) - VCC3V3 → VCC3V3_EQ 使用 TPS22918 负载开关（[证据 11] 第 “VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ” 段，[证据 20] 表 ) - VCC3V3 → VCC3V3_ETH 使用负载开关方案（[证据 11] 第 “VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH” 段），但未给出具体型号。 - 原理图网络连接显示 VCC3V3 直接连接到上述电源器件的电源输入引脚（[证据 2] 中 U1800.VIN、U1804.VIN、U1700.VIN 等分别对应各方案，[证据 5] 中 U1802.VOUT 证实 VCC3V3_EQ 输出由 TPS22918 产生）。 **缺失信息** - 用于 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关型号未在文档中提供，仅知方案为负载开关，原理图中 U1500 可能承担该功能但无型号证据。

ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F12</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，基于LN10046设计，给系统提供电源。 The KL30 to VCC3V3 is a first-level DC-DC based on the LN10046 design and provides power to the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用LN10046电源DCDC芯片，将12V系统电源转换为3.3V，提供给系统供电使用。 The LN10046 power supply DCDC chip is used to convert the 12V system power supply to 3.3V and provide power supply to the system for use. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：H7; None：VDD; None：VDD; None：POWER; None：VCC1V25_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：1V25供电，MCU核电源域; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：N19; None：VDD; None：VDD; None：POWER; None：VCC1V25_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：1V25供电，MCU核电源域; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 HW Interface Component ：B; None：F12/POWER; None：LN10046输出的VCC3V3，经过磁珠转换为VCC3V3_MCU，用于给MCU的3V3电源域供电。; MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：B18; None：VDDP3; None：VDDP3; None：POWER; None：VCC3V3_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：3V3供电，MCU内部Flash供电; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：A19; None：VDDP3; None：VDDP3; None：POWER; None：VCC3V3_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：3V3供电，MCU内部Flash供电; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：A2; None：VEXT; None：VEXT; None：POWER; None：VCC3V3_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：3V3供电，External Power Supply; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：B3; None：VEXT; None：VEXT; None：POWER; None：VCC3V3_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：3V3供电，External Power Supply; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F15</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR应用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用SA23002电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为0V75_MIPI_DDR，提供给EQ6的IO口供电。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 SA23002 power chip is used to convert VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR to supply power to the I/O port of EQ6. 问题：软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节？
参考答案：应查 HSI 7.2 节“LP87702 配置”，其中给出软件寄存器和 I²C 配置说明。
实际回答：软件配置 LP87702 应查看 HSI 文档的 **第7.2节“LP87702配置”**（LP87702 Configuration）。 - 支撑证据：HSI 文档目录中明确列出“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”（证据[3]）；该节正文开头指出“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示”（证据[2]），说明该节包含了软件配置内容。
评分正文：软件配置 LP87702 应查看 HSI 文档的 **第7.2节“LP87702配置”**（LP87702 Configuration）。 - 支撑证据：HSI 文档目录中明确列出“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”（证据[3]）；该节正文开头指出“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示”（证据[2]），说明该节包含了软件配置内容。

READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | None：U20; 问题：网表写 TC367、接口文档写 TC377，这两个说法怎么处理？
参考答案：必须保留来源差异：EDF 的 U900 型号是 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，而 HSI 章节名是 TC377 最小系统；现有证据不足以把二者无条件视为同一准确型号。
实际回答：根据现有证据，网表（电路设计文件）和接口文档对于主控芯片型号的描述存在不一致，网表指向TC367，接口文档指向TC377，两者为不同器件型号，无法认定为同一器件或简单笔误。 **直接结论：网表和接口文档描述了不同的芯片型号，需要进一步核实以确定哪个为正确版本。** **支撑证据：** 1. **网表中的TC367上下文：** * **来源：** `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 电路设计文件。 * **证据：** 该网表中存在多个以 **TC367** 命名的网络，如 `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TDI`、`TC367_RESET_N` 等（证据[1]至[6]）。同时，该网表中明确包含两个实例 `U900A` 和 `U900B`，其型号均为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S`（证据[7]和[8]）。这表明网表设计是基于 **TC367** 芯片。 2. **接口文档中的TC377上下文：** * **来源：** `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 接口文档。 * **证据：** 该文档的目录中明确列出“TC377最小系统”章节（证据[9]）。更具体地，在文档正文中，`F01` 模块功能描述为“32位MCU芯片**TC377**最小系统模块”，并详细说明其主控芯片为 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`（证据[18]）。这表明接口文档描述的是基于 **TC377** 芯片的设计。 3. **TC367与TC377的对比：** * **来源：** 综合上述电路设计文件和接口文档。 * **证据：** 网表中使用的具体芯片型号为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S`（证据[7], [8]），而接口文档中使用的具体芯片型号为 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`（证据[18]）。这两个型号在部件号（Part Number）上存在明显差异（TC367 vs TC377），属于不同的器件。现有证据中不存在任何一份资料将这两个型号等同或证明其兼容互换。 **缺失信息：** * **无法判断哪个是正确版本：** 基于现有证据，无法确定网表（TC367）和接口文档（TC377）哪个是项目实际采用的正确设计。没有证据表明两者之间存在笔误或等同关系，也没有额外资料（如物料清单、设计评审记录）可以裁定此冲突。 * **缺乏兼容性证据：** 没有证据（如datasheet）表明 `TC367` 和 `TC377` 在引脚、封装或功能上完全兼容，因此不能假设它们可以互相替换。
评分正文：根据现有证据，网表（电路设计文件）和接口文档对于主控芯片型号的描述存在不一致，网表指向TC367，接口文档指向TC377，两者为不同器件型号，无法认定为同一器件或简单笔误。 **直接结论：网表和接口文档描述了不同的芯片型号，需要进一步核实以确定哪个为正确版本。** **支撑证据：** 1. **网表中的TC367上下文：** * **来源：** `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 电路设计文件。 * **证据：** 该网表中存在多个以 **TC367** 命名的网络，如 `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TDI`、`TC367_RESET_N` 等（证据[1]至[6]）。同时，该网表中明确包含两个实例 `U900A` 和 `U900B`，其型号均为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S`（证据[7]和[8]）。这表明网表设计是基于 **TC367** 芯片。 2. **接口文档中的TC377上下文：** * **来源：** `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 接口文档。 * **证据：** 该文档的目录中明确列出“TC377最小系统”章节（证据[9]）。更具体地，在文档正文中，`F01` 模块功能描述为“32位MCU芯片**TC377**最小系统模块”，并详细说明其主控芯片为 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`（证据[18]）。这表明接口文档描述的是基于 **TC377** 芯片的设计。 3. **TC367与TC377的对比：** * **来源：** 综合上述电路设计文件和接口文档。 * **证据：** 网表中使用的具体芯片型号为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S`（证据[7], [8]），而接口文档中使用的具体芯片型号为 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`（证据[18]）。这两个型号在部件号（Part Number）上存在明显差异（TC367 vs TC377），属于不同的器件。现有证据中不存在任何一份资料将这两个型号等同或证明其兼容互换。 **缺失信息：** * **无法判断哪个是正确版本：** 基于现有证据，无法确定网表（TC367）和接口文档（TC377）哪个是项目实际采用的正确设计。没有证据表明两者之间存在笔误或等同关系，也没有额外资料（如物料清单、设计评审记录）可以裁定此冲突。 * **缺乏兼容性证据：** 没有证据（如datasheet）表明 `TC367` 和 `TC377` 在引脚、封装或功能上完全兼容，因此不能假设它们可以互相替换。

Purpose软硬接口文档的目的是描述软件和硬件的交互，并且确保软件硬件和TSR的一致性。The goal of the hardware software interface document is to specify the hardware and software interaction and be consistent with the technical safety concept.适用范围 Range of Application此文档适用于奇瑞EQ6L T1G ADAS项目的功能安全开发全过程。This document is applied for the whole functional safety development process of EQ6L T1G ADAS project.读者对象 TReader项目经理Project <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN NRST电压低于阈值。LP87702-Q1器件停止工作。 READ OTP 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO电平，并且NRST电压高于阈值电平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平和偏置电路被启用。OTP配置被加载到寄存器。 STANDBY 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平、控制和偏压电路被启用。所有的寄存器都可以被主机处理器通过系统串口读写。电压转换器可以根据需要进行使能输出。 ACTIVE 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。稳压DC/DC变换器处于使能状态。所有的寄存器都可以被MCU通过系统串口读写。 SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F27</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>上电模式、下电模式、运行模式 Power on mode, power off mode, run mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 上电模式 一级电源VCC3V3_MCU输出有效后，MCU的内置驱动器与外置的MOS/电感/电容实现3V3_MCU转1V25_MCU的BUCK电源。 2、 运行模式 MCU的内置驱动器与外置的MOS/电感/电容构成的BUCK电源正常输出VCC1V25_MCU，用于MCU核供电。 3、 下电模式 一级电源VCC3V3_MCU输出掉电后，MCU的内置驱动器开始关断VCC1V25_MCU。 1.Power on mode After the output of VCC3V3_MCU is effective, the built-in driver of MCU and the external MOS/ inductor/capacitor realize the BUCK power from 3V3 MCU to 1V25 MCU. 2.Run mode MCU built-in driver and external MOS/ inductor/capacitor BUCK power supply composed of normal output VCC1V25 MCU for MCU core power supply. 3.Power off mode After the output power of the VCC3V3_MCU fails, the built-in driver of the MCU starts to shut down the VCC1V25 MCU.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | 数量统计：70;
