TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F2/F3/F4/F28</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>上电模式、下电模式、运行模式、复位模式 Power on mode, Power off mode, Run mode,Reset mode</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、上电模式 MCU按照时序控制EQ6的电源VCC3V3_EQ、VCC1V8、VCC1V8_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V1上电和复位信号EYEQ6_RESET_N配置为高电平。 2、下电模式 MCU按照时序控制EQ6的电源VCC3V3_EQ、VCC1V8、VCC1V8_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V1下电，复位信号EYEQ6_RESET_N配置为低电平。 3、运行模式 EQ6系统工作，实时处理CAM图像数据并发送给MCU。 4、复位模式 MCU控制复位信号EYEQ6_RESET_N配置为低电平，EQ6进入复位状态。 Power on mode MCU controls EQ6 power supply VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V8_EQ, VCCOV75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V1 power on and RESET signal EYEQ6 RESET_N to high level according to timing. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F14</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>上电模式、下电模式、运行模式 Power on mode, power off mode, run mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 上电模式 使能信号MCU_VCC0V75_EN=H，二级电源TPS62872开始工作，为负载提供VCC0V75的电源。 2、 下电模式 使能信号MCU_VCC0V75_EN=L后，二级电源TPS62872停止VCC0V75供电。 3、 运行模式 二级电源TPS62872正常输出VCC0V75，系统正常工作。 Power on mode The enabling signal MCU VCC0V75 EN-H, the secondary power supply TPS62872 starts to work and supplies the power to VCC0V75 for the load. 评分上下文：4/30 段，1422/22264 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-joint-pmic-noisy-querysuccess0.1660.4800.286not_applicable0.0000.0000.5000.357not_applicable
问题：忽略无关的摄像头和 CAN 内容，只结合电路与 HSI 说明 LP87702 的供电输出和配置。
参考答案：LP87702-Q1 是 U1700，输入 VCC3V3，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50；配置依据 HSI 7.2 节。
实际回答：### 直接结论 - **供电输出参数**：**无直接证据**。当前电路设计（`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）仅记录了 LP87702‑Q1 的器件型号（`LP87702RRHBRQ1`）和封装，未提供任何输出引脚、电压、电流等参数；HSI 文档（`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）及 datasheet 片段均未给出具体的输出值。**缺口：缺少供电输出引脚、电压、电流的明确数据。** - **配置信息**：**有证据**。HSI 文档定义了四种操作模式（SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE），并说明模式切换依赖于 NRST 和 VANA 电压阈值；datasheet 提供了 CONTROL1~CONTROL3 寄存器的字段描述，涵盖软件使能、强制 PWM、输出放电、打嗝模式、电压斜坡、电压范围、软启动时间、单操作、PG 消隐等配置项。此外，VSEL 引脚用于选择启动电压，FSEL 引脚选择开关频率，I2C 接口可读写寄存器。 ### 分项说明 #### 1. 供电输出参数（子问题 sq_1）—— **缺口** - **电路设计证据**：实例 `U1700` 为 `LP87702-Q1`，型号 `LP87702RRHBRQ1`，封装 `pqfn50p5x5x90-32`（证据[8]）。但图中仅显示模块间的 GND 连接及 I2C 信号线（`MCU_SCL_P15.4`、`MCU_SDA_P15.4`）（证据[1]–[7]），**未标识任何供电输出引脚、电压值或电流限值**。 - **HSI 文档证据**：文档 `ea7b2234534382bf` 描述了操作模式，但**未提及具体输出电压或电流**（证据[12]）。 - **缺失信息**：无法确认 LP87702 的供电输出引脚（如 VOUT1、VOUT2、VOUT3）、各通道输出电压（如 0.75V、1.8V 等）或额定电流。**必须补充电路设计细节或器件 datasheet 中的输出规格。** #### 2. 配置信息（子问题 sq_2）—— **已覆盖** - **操作模式与使能条件**（来源：HSI 文档 `ea7b2234534382bf`，证据[12]）： - `SHUTDOWN`：NRST 电压低于阈值，器件停止工作；或使能信号 `MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN` 为低时次级电源停止。 - `READ OTP`：VANA 高于 VANAUVLO 且 NRST 高于阈值，电压转换器禁用，OTP 加载至寄存器。 - `STANDBY`：VANA 高于 VANAUVLO 且 NRST 高于阈值，电压转换器禁用，所有寄存器可读写，可依需使能输出。 - `ACTIVE`：VANA 高于 VANAUVLO 且 NRST 高于阈值，DC/DC 转换器使能，寄存器可读写。 - **寄存器配置**（来源：datasheet `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`）： - **CONTROL1**（证据[18]）：`SWEN`（软件使能）、`FPWMEN`（强制 PWM）、`DISCHEN`（输出放电）、`HICCUPEN`（打嗝模式使能）、`VRAMP[1:0]`（电压斜坡速率：0.5/1.25/5/10 mV/μs）。 - **CONTROL2**（证据[13]）：`VRANGE[1:0]`（输出电压范围选择，如 0.4~1.675V 步进 5mV）、`SSTIME[1:0]`（软启动时间：0.5/1/2/4 ms）。 - **CONTROL3**（证据[11]）：`SINGLE`（单操作，控制 EN 下拉和 SYNCOUT）、`PGBLNKDVS`（DVS 期间 PG 消隐）。 - **其他配置手段**（来源：datasheet 文本，证据[16]、[19]、[20]）： - `VSEL` 引脚选择启动电压，`FSEL` 引脚选择开关频率（1.5/2.25/2.5/3.0 MHz 或外部同步）。 - 可通过 I2C 接口在启动前或运行中修改电压设置；DVS 时受 `VRAMP` 控制。 - 堆叠操作时，主器件 VSEL 设置默认电压，I2C 地址由启动电压选项决定。 - **使能信号**：EN 引脚（电平控制）和 I2C 寄存器 `SWEN` 位共同控制；NRST 和 VANA 电压阈值作为模式切换前提（证据[12]）。
评分正文：### 直接结论 - **供电输出参数**：**无直接证据**。当前电路设计（`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）仅记录了 LP87702‑Q1 的器件型号（`LP87702RRHBRQ1`）和封装，未提供任何输出引脚、电压、电流等参数；HSI 文档（`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）及 datasheet 片段均未给出具体的输出值。**缺口：缺少供电输出引脚、电压、电流的明确数据。** - **配置信息**：**有证据**。HSI 文档定义了四种操作模式（SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE），并说明模式切换依赖于 NRST 和 VANA 电压阈值；datasheet 提供了 CONTROL1~CONTROL3 寄存器的字段描述，涵盖软件使能、强制 PWM、输出放电、打嗝模式、电压斜坡、电压范围、软启动时间、单操作、PG 消隐等配置项。此外，VSEL 引脚用于选择启动电压，FSEL 引脚选择开关频率，I2C 接口可读写寄存器。 ### 分项说明 #### 1. 供电输出参数（子问题 sq_1）—— **缺口** - **电路设计证据**：实例 `U1700` 为 `LP87702-Q1`，型号 `LP87702RRHBRQ1`，封装 `pqfn50p5x5x90-32`（证据[8]）。但图中仅显示模块间的 GND 连接及 I2C 信号线（`MCU_SCL_P15.4`、`MCU_SDA_P15.4`）（证据[1]–[7]），**未标识任何供电输出引脚、电压值或电流限值**。 - **HSI 文档证据**：文档 `ea7b2234534382bf` 描述了操作模式，但**未提及具体输出电压或电流**（证据[12]）。 - **缺失信息**：无法确认 LP87702 的供电输出引脚（如 VOUT1、VOUT2、VOUT3）、各通道输出电压（如 0.75V、1.8V 等）或额定电流。**必须补充电路设计细节或器件 datasheet 中的输出规格。** #### 2. 配置信息（子问题 sq_2）—— **已覆盖** - **操作模式与使能条件**（来源：HSI 文档 `ea7b2234534382bf`，证据[12]）： - `SHUTDOWN`：NRST 电压低于阈值，器件停止工作；或使能信号 `MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN` 为低时次级电源停止。 - `READ OTP`：VANA 高于 VANAUVLO 且 NRST 高于阈值，电压转换器禁用，OTP 加载至寄存器。 - `STANDBY`：VANA 高于 VANAUVLO 且 NRST 高于阈值，电压转换器禁用，所有寄存器可读写，可依需使能输出。 - `ACTIVE`：VANA 高于 VANAUVLO 且 NRST 高于阈值，DC/DC 转换器使能，寄存器可读写。 - **寄存器配置**（来源：datasheet `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`）： - **CONTROL1**（证据[18]）：`SWEN`（软件使能）、`FPWMEN`（强制 PWM）、`DISCHEN`（输出放电）、`HICCUPEN`（打嗝模式使能）、`VRAMP[1:0]`（电压斜坡速率：0.5/1.25/5/10 mV/μs）。 - **CONTROL2**（证据[13]）：`VRANGE[1:0]`（输出电压范围选择，如 0.4~1.675V 步进 5mV）、`SSTIME[1:0]`（软启动时间：0.5/1/2/4 ms）。 - **CONTROL3**（证据[11]）：`SINGLE`（单操作，控制 EN 下拉和 SYNCOUT）、`PGBLNKDVS`（DVS 期间 PG 消隐）。 - **其他配置手段**（来源：datasheet 文本，证据[16]、[19]、[20]）： - `VSEL` 引脚选择启动电压，`FSEL` 引脚选择开关频率（1.5/2.25/2.5/3.0 MHz 或外部同步）。 - 可通过 I2C 接口在启动前或运行中修改电压设置；DVS 时受 `VRAMP` 控制。 - 堆叠操作时，主器件 VSEL 设置默认电压，I2C 地址由启动电压选项决定。 - **使能信号**：EN 引脚（电平控制）和 I2C 寄存器 `SWEN` 位共同控制；NRST 和 VANA 电压阈值作为模式切换前提（证据[12]）。

READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. (3) | Vin is the input voltage Vout is the output voltage.· · L is the effective inductance at the peak current level. tpd is the propagation delay of the current limit comparator (typically 50 ns). 9.3.13.2HiccupMode To enable hiccup operation, ensure that HICCUPEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. If hiccup operation is enabled and the high-side switch current exceeds the current limit threshold on 32 consecutive switching cycles, the device: Stops switching for 128 clock cycles, after which it automatically starts switching again (it starts a new soft-start sequence) · Sets the HICCUP bit in the STATUS register Pulls the PG pin low. The PG pin stays low until the overload condition goes away and the device can start up correctly and regulate the output voltage. Note that power-good function has a deglitch circuit, which delays the rising edge of the power-good signal by 40 μs (typical). Hiccup operation continues in a repeating sequence of 32 cycles in current limit, followed by a pause of 128 cycles, followed by a soft-start attempt for as long as the output overload condition exists. The device clears the HICCUP bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition has been removed. 9.3.13.3CurrentLimitMode To enable current limit mode, ensure that HICCUPEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. When current limit operation is enabled, the device limits the high-side switch current cycle-by-cycle for as long as the overload condition exists. If the device limits the high-side switch current for four or more consecutive switching cycles, the device sets ILIM = 1 in the STATUS register. The device clears the ILIM bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition no longer exits. 9.3.14 Power Good (PG) The power good (PG) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: · In a standalone configuration and in the primary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an open- drain output that indicates the status of the converter or stack. · In a secondary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an input that indicates when the soft-start sequence is complete and all converters in the stack can change from DCM switching to CCM switching. 9.3.14.1 Standalone orPrimaryDeviceBehavior The primary purpose of the PG pin is to indicate if the output voltage is in regulation, but it also indicates if the device is in thermal shutdown or disabled. Table 9-7 summarizes the behavior of the PG pin in a standalone or primary device. | <table><caption> Table 9-14. Then, when the user pulls the EN pin high, the device starts up with the desired start-up voltage. Note that if the user changes the device settings through I?C while the device is ramping, the device ignores the changes until the ramp is complete 9.3.6.4 Dynamic Voltage Scaling If the user changes the output voltage setpoint while the DC/DC converter is operating, the device ramps up or down to the new voltage setting in a controlled way. The VRAMP[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register sets the slew rate when the device ramps from one voltage to another during DVS (see Table 9-6). Note that ramping the output to a higher voltage requires additional output current, so that during DvS, the converter must generate a total output current given by: dVour lour = lour c) + Cour dVOouT where · lout is the total current the converter must generate while ramping to a higher voltage. louT(Dc) is the DC load current.· Cout is the total output capacitance. dVout/dt is the slew rate of the output voltage (programmable in the range 0.5 mV/μs to 10 mV/us). For correct operation, ensure that the total output current during DVS does not exceed the current limit of the device. 9.3.7 Compensation (CoMP) The CoMP pin is the connection point for an external compensation network. A series-connected resistor and capacitor to GOSNS is sufficient for typical applications. The series-connected resistor also provides enough scope to optimize the loop response for a wide range of operating conditions. When using multiple devices in a stacked configuration, all devices share a common compensation network, and the COMP pin makes sure there is equal current sharing between them (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.8 ModeSelection and Clock Synchronization (MODE/SYNC) A high level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects forced PWM operation. A low level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects power save operation, in which, the device automatically transitions between PWM and PFM, according to the load conditions. | If the junction temperature falls below the thermal shutdown threshold of 150°C (typical), the device: · Starts switching again, starting with a new soft-start sequence · Sets the EN pin to high impedance ·Sets the PG pin to high-impedance The device clears the TSHUT bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TSHUT threshold of 150°C (typical). In a stacked configuration, in which all devices share a common enable signal, a thermal shutdown condition in one device disables the entire stack. When the hot device cools down, the whole stack automatically starts switching again. 9.3.17 Stacked Operation The user can connect multiple devices in parallel in what is known as a "stack"; for example, to increase output current capability or reduce device junction temperature. A stack comprises one primary device and one or more secondary devices. During initialization, each device monitors its SYNC_OUT pin to determine if must operate as a primary device or a secondary device: · If there is a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground, the device operates as a secondary device. · If the SYNC_OUT pin is high impedance, the device operates as a primary device. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The key points to note are: All the devices in the stack share a common enable signal, which must be pulled up with a resistance of at least 15 kQ. All the devices in the stack share a common power-good signal. All the devices in the stack share a common compensation signal. All secondary devices must connect a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground. The remote sense pins (VOSNS and GOSNS) of each device must be connected (do not leave these pins floating). Each device must be configured for the same switching frequency.· The primary device must be configured for forced PwM operation (secondary devices are automatically configured for forcedPwM operation). A stacked configuration can support synchronization to an external clock or spread-spectrum clocking.· Only the VSEL pin of the primary device is used to set the default output voltage. The VSEL pin of secondary· devices is not used and must be connected to ground. The SDA and SCL pins of secondary devices are not used and must be connected to ground. | ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition thresholdWCCA计算结果如下，软件应按照如下过欠压阈值设置：The WCCA calculation results are as follows. Fault conditions have no effect on the EN pin when SINGLE = 1 (the EN pin is always pulled down during device initialization). In stacked applications, ensure that SINGLE = 0. When the internal SYSTEM_READY signal is low (that is, initialization is complete and there are no fault conditions), the internal open-drain transistor is high impedance and the EN pin functions like a standard input. A high level on the EN pin enables the DC/DC converter in the device. A low level disables the DC/DC converter (the I2C interface is enabled as soon as the device has completed its initialization and is not affected by the state of the internal ENABLE or SYSTEM_READY signals). A low level on the EN pin forces the device into shutdown. During shutdown, the MOSFETs in the power stage are off, the internal control circuitry is disabled, and the device consumes only 20 μA (typical). The rising threshold voltage of the EN pin is 1.0 V and the falling threshold voltage is 0.9 V. The tolerance of the threshold voltages is ±30 mV, which means that the user can use the EN pin to implement precise turn-on and turn-off behavior. When power is applied to the VIN pin, the toggling of the EN pin does not reset the loaded default register settings. 9.3.4 Start-Up When the voltage on the VIN pin exceeds the positive-going UVLO threshold, the device initializes as follows: ·The device pulls the EN pin low. ·The device enables the internal reference voltage. · The device reads the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. · The device loads the default values into the device registers. When initialization is complete, the device enables I²C communication and releases the EN pin. The external circuitry controlling the EN pin now determines the behavior of the device: · If the EN pin is low, the device is disabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers, but the DC/DC converter does not operate · If the EN pin is high, the device is enabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers and, after a short delay, the DC/DC converter starts to ramp up its output. The SSTIME[1:0] bits in the CONTROL2 register select the duration of the soft-start ramp: | all operating conditions. Page Changes from Revision A (July 2022) to Revision B (January 2023) | 9.4.4 0n The device operates in on mode when the supply voltage is greater than the UVLO threshold and all of the following conditions are true: ·A high-level is applied to the EN pin. The bus consists of a data line (SDA) and a clock line (SCL) with pullup structures. When the bus is idle, both SDA and SCL lines are pulled high. All I2C-compatible devices connect to the I2C bus through open-drain I/O pins, SDA and SCL. A controller, usually a microcontroller or a digital signal processor, controls the bus. The controller is responsible for generating the SCL signal and device addresses. The controller also generates specific conditions that indicate the START and STOP of data transfer. A target receives data, transmits data, or both on the bus under control of the controller. The TPS6287x-Q1 device operates as a target and supports the following data transfer modes, as defined in the I2C-Bus Specification: standard mode (100 kbps), fast mode (400 kbps), and fast mode plus (1 Mbps). The interface adds flexibility to the power supply solution, enabling most functions to be programmed to new values depending on the instantaneous application requirements. Register contents remain intact as long as the input voltage remains above 1.4 V. The data transfer protocol for standard and fast modes is exactly the same, therefore, they are referred to as F/S-mode in this document. The device supports 7-bit addressing; general call addresses are not supported. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F19</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>上电模式、下电模式、运行模式 Power on mode, Power off mode, Run mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 上电模式 MCU通过将MCU_1V8_EQ_EN信号配置为逻辑高使能VCC1V8_EQ电源输出，用于EQ系统供电。 2、 下电模式 MCU通过将MCU_1V8_EQ_EN信号配置为逻辑低关闭VCC1V8_EQ电源输出。 3、 运行模式 TPS22918正常输出VCC1V8_EQ。 Power on mode The MCU is used to power the EQ system by configuring the MCU-1 V8-EQ_EN signal to the logical high enable VCCIV8-EQ power output. 问题：从 MCU 使能开始，追踪到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。
参考答案：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN 使能 U1500，VCC3V3 经 U1500 形成 VCC3V3_ETH，再进入 U1501 TPS74501，输出 VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：### 结论 从 MCU 使能到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径**无法完全追踪**，存在两个关键缺口： 1. **MCU 使能信号的具体网络和连接的外部器件未知**（控制 U1500 的 `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN` 信号来源未在证据中显示）。 2. **以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源引脚未知**（VCC1V0_ETH 网络未与 PHY 器件 U1400 的电源引脚直接连接）。 以下为基于现有证据的局部路径和缺失信息。 --- ### 子问题 1：MCU 的使能信号连接到了哪个电源管理 IC 或开关？ **直接结论**：存在一个受控于 `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN` 信号的电源开关 U1500，但该信号的 MCU 来源未在证据中给出。 **支撑证据**： - 电源拓扑 `power_path` 描述：“VCC3V3 -> U1500 (20181204001), controls: MCU_3V3_ETH_EN -> VCC3V3_ETH”（[1]）。 - 证据中未出现网络名 `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN` 的连接信息（未在 `net` 或 `pin_mapping` 中列出）。 **缺失信息**： - `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN` 信号来自 MCU 的哪个引脚，或是否经过其他中间电路。 - 无法确定 MCU 使能信号是否直接连接到 U1500。 --- ### 子问题 2：该电源管理 IC 的输出电压和路径是？如何连接到以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源？ **直接结论**：U1500 输出 `VCC3V3_ETH`，该电压作为 U1501 的供电输入和使能信号，U1501 输出 `VCC1V0_ETH`。但 `VCC1V0_ETH` 未明确连接到以太网 PHY 的电源引脚。 **支撑证据**： - U1500 输出 `VCC3V3_ETH`（[1]）。 - U1501 的引脚映射：`IN -> VCC3V3_ETH`，`EN -> VCC3V3_ETH`，`OUT -> VCC1V0_ETH`（[3]）。 - 网络 `VCC1V0_ETH` 连接了 `C1420, C1506, C1507, L1403, R1146, R1504, TP1503, U1501.OUT`，但未连接到以太网 PHY 器件 U1400（[14]）。 **缺失信息**： - 以太网 PHY（U1400）的 1.0V 电源引脚（如 `VDD`、`VCC` 等）未在证据中列出。 - 没有直连关系表明 `VCC1V0_ETH` 供给 U1400。 --- ### 子问题 3：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源引脚是什么？其供电路径上的中间节点（如 LDO、DC-DC、滤波器等）有哪些？ **直接结论**：无法确定以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源引脚；供电路径上的中间节点已知有 U1501（LDO/DC-DC）和滤波元件，但终点不明确。 **支撑证据**： - 中间节点：U1501 输出 `VCC1V0_ETH`，网络包含磁珠/电感 `L1403`、电容 `C1420/C1506/C1507`、电阻 `R1146/R1504`（[14]）。 - 以太网 PHY（U1400）的引脚映射仅涉及信号引脚 `TRD_M/TRD_P/RESET_N/WAKE`（[5][6][7][8]），未提供电源引脚名称或连接。 **缺失信息**： - U1400 的 1.0V 电源引脚（如 `VDDIO`、`VDD_CORE` 等）未在证据中描述。 - 无法确认 `VCC1V0_ETH` 是否直接供给 U1400，或经过其他中间节点。 --- ### 总结：完整路径的已知与缺失 | 路径环节 | 已知内容 | 缺失内容 | |----------|----------|----------| | MCU 使能信号 | 信号名 `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN` 控制 U1500 | 信号来源（MCU 引脚）未给出 | | 中间开关 U1500 | 输入 VCC3V3，输出 VCC3V3_ETH | 无 | | 中间 LDO/DC-DC U1501 | 输入/使能 VCC3V3_ETH，输出 VCC1V0_ETH | 无 | | 滤波/连接元件 | L1403、C1420、C1506、C1507、R1146、R1504 | 无 | | 以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源引脚 | 未提供 | 电源引脚名称及与 VCC1V0_ETH 的连接均缺失 | **注意**：证据中提到的 U1700 及其使能信号 `MCU_5V_EN` 产生 VCC50，但该路径与以太网 1.0V 电源无关，不构成追踪路径的一部分。
